  # Patch Center Frontend — Feature & Behavior Spec (for React/TypeScript
  rewrite)

  **Base directory:** `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop\static\`
  All `file:line` anchors below are relative to that base. Primary files:
  `patches.html`, `patches.js`, `prompts_novice.js`, plus shared `common.js`,
  `node_core.js`, `sidenav.js`, `copybox.js`, `folderpick.js`, and the
  referenced detail page `adapt_run.html`.

  ---

  ## 1. Page Shell, Layout, Navigation

  ### 1.1 Overall shell (`patches.html`)

  - Fixed left sidebar `#patchSidenav` (`patches.html:14`) — intentionally
  empty; `/sidenav.js` renders items from server-provided menu permissions
  (`patches.html:16-17`). Collapse persisted in
  `localStorage['cc-web-patch-sidenav']` (`patches.html:9`, `sidenav.js:22`).
  - Top bar (`patches.html:20-36`):
    - Brand `⚡ CC-Web`.
    - App-mode toggle: "聊天" links to `/chat.html`; "智能开发" is the current
  page.
    - `#patchUsageStats` (hidden until loaded): online users + cumulative usage
  counts.
    - `#patchApiStatus`: "未连接" / "已连接" / "连接失败" / "配置缺失".
    - `#patchCurrentUser`: `display_name (role)`.
    - Buttons: `#patchUserSettings`, `#patchUpdateCheck`, `#patchHelpManual`,
  `#patchLogout` — all hidden until authenticated; `#patchThemeToggle` always
  visible.
  - Update banner `#patchUpdateBanner` (`patches.html:39-46`) with 更新说明 /
  立即更新 / 忽略.
  - Auth area (`patches.html:48-62`):
    - `#patchAuthLoading` + `#patchAuthLoadingText` + retry button
  (`#patchAuthRetry`).
    - `#patchLoginCard` with username/password/login and `#patchLoginMessage`.
    - `#patchAuthLayout` (hidden until login) containing all tabs/panels.
  - Pre-auth HTML adds `patch-auth` + `patch-auth-pending` classes if a token
  exists (`patches.html:10`); the pending class is removed after auth resolution
  (`patches.js:309`, `patches.js:5421`).

  # cc-web Rust Backend API Contract

  Repository root: `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop`

  Sources inspected: `src/main.rs`, `src/models.rs`, `src/api/*.rs`,
  `src/ai/streaming.rs`, `src/ai/{claude,pi,codex}.rs`, `src/recorder.rs`,
  `src/static_files.rs`, `src/logging.rs`, `src/claude_history.rs`, and the
  existing frontend (`static/common.js`, `app.js`, `node_core.js`, `novice*.js`,
  `patches.js`).

  ---

  ## 1. Runtime facts that shape the frontend contract

  | Fact | Value |
  |---|---|
  | Dev bind | `0.0.0.0:3030`, WEB_URL `http://localhost:3030`
  (`src/main.rs:191-199`) |
  | implCon bind | `0.0.0.0:3031`, WEB_URL `http://127.0.0.1:3031`
  (`src/main.rs:191-199`) |
  | Data dir | dev `~/.cc-web`, implCon `~/.cc-web-novice`
  (`src/main.rs:202-205`) |
  | Portable temp dir | `<directory containing cc-web.exe>/temp`
  (`src/main.rs:76-89`) |
  | Feature gate | `implCon` is the "implementation/novice" build; absence of it
  is the dev build (`Cargo.toml:6-12`) |
  | CORS | Dev: allow any origin/method/header, max-age 3600. implCon: **no CORS
  middleware at all** (`src/main.rs:348-360`) |
  | Auth | cc-web itself has **no authentication, no cookies, no
  sessions/tokens**. All `/api/*` are open. See section 12 |
  | Body limits | `PayloadConfig` raised to 500 MB for multipart
  (`src/main.rs:346-347`); JSON uses actix defaults (not overridden) |
  | Unknown path | `default_service` is the static-file handler, so unregistered
  `/api/*` returns **404 plain text `Not Found`**, not JSON
  (`src/main.rs:437-438`, `src/static_files.rs:100-102`) |

  Mixed envelope conventions are important for the TypeScript rewrite:

  - Most endpoints return `{ "success": true/false, ... }` (agent, files,
  update, adapt, recorder, attachments).
  - Session endpoints often return bare objects/`{ "error": string }` (no
  `success`).
  - Node-run endpoints use `{ "code": 0, "data": ... }`.
  - `/api/patch-config` and `/api/debug/state` have no envelope at all.
  - Adapt start uses `run_id` snake_case; agent endpoints use `sessionId`
  camelCase.

  ---

  | Method | Path | Transport | Purpose |

  | GET | `/api/models` | JSON | Models for an assistant |

  |

  |

  ## 3. Shared data types

  `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/models.rs:6-53`

  ```ts
  interface Session {
    id: string;
    assistant: string;            // "claude" | "pi" | "codex"
    cwd: string;
    model: string;
    messages: Message[];
    created_at: string;           // RFC3339, serde chrono
    updated_at: string;
    history_context?: string;     // omitted when null
    name?: string;                // omitted when null
    agent_session_id?: string;    // Claude native session id
    history_already_sent?: boolean; // omitted when false
  }

  interface Message {
    role: string;                 // "user" | "assistant" | "system"
    content: string;
    timestamp: number;            // epoch milliseconds
    content_blocks?: ContentBlock[]; // omitted when null
    assistant?: string;           // omitted when null
  }

  type ContentBlock =
    | { type: "text"; text: string }
    | { type: "thinking"; thinking: string }
    | { type: "tool_use"; id: string; name: string; input: unknown }
    | { type: "tool_result"; tool_use_id: string; content: string };
  ```

  Request structs: `NewSessionRequest`, `StartPromptRequest`, `CommandRequest`,
  `SwitchAssistantRequest` at `src/models.rs:55-85`.

  ## 4. Meta / configuration endpoints

  ### 4.1 GET `/api/version`
  Handler: `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/api/update.rs:94-127`

  No request. Returns local binary metadata (computes SHA-256 of the running exe
  on every call).

  ```ts
  // 200
  { success: true, data: {
    version: string;          // Cargo package version, e.g. "1.0.5"
    marker: string;           // "CCWEB-VERSION:1.0.5" or
  "CCWEB-VERSION:novice-1.0.5"
    build_unix: string;       // build epoch seconds, as string
    edition: "dev" | "novice";
    platform: "windows" | "macos-arm64" | "macos-x64" | "";
    exe_path: string;
    exe_mtime: number;        // epoch seconds
    size: number;             // bytes
    sha256: string;
  }}
  // 500
  { success: false, error: string }
  ```

  // 200

  ### 4.5 GET `/api/models?assistant=<name>`
  Handler: `src/api/models.rs:19-74`

  Query parsing is manual and only recognizes `assistant`; omitted/unknown
  assistant falls back to the default assistant (first registered, `claude`).
  Unknown assistant name returns 400.

  ```ts
  // 200
  {
    models: Record<string, string>;      // id -> name
    model_list: Array<{ id: string; name: string; provider: string }>;
    default_model: { provider: string; model_id: string }; // provider is the
  assistant name
  }
  // 400
  { error: "Assistant not found" }
  ```

  Model sources:
  - Claude: `~/.claude/settings.json` env keys `ANTHROPIC_MODEL`,
  `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_{SONNET,OPUS,HAIKU}_MODEL`, plus `model`; fallback
  `[{id:"unknown",name:"Unknown",provider:"anthropic"}]`
  (`src/ai/claude.rs:243-288, 417-433`).
  - Pi: `~/.pi/agent/models.json`; fallback MiMo/Sonnet/Opus
  (`src/ai/pi.rs:280-331`).
  - Codex: `models_cache.json`; fallback `unknown/unknown`
  (`src/ai/codex.rs:169-185`).

  ### 4.6 GET `/api/env-check?home=<optional dir>`
  Handler: `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/api/env_check.rs:124-211`

  Runs `claude --version`, `python --version`, `java -version`/`JAVA_HOME`,
  finds Git Bash and shallow-scans `home` for a JDK. No request-level timeout;
  the comment expects a frontend fetch timeout.

  ```ts
  // 200 always (unless the extractor itself fails)
  {
    success: true;
    claude:  { ok: boolean; detail: string };
    python:  { ok: boolean; detail: string };
    java:    { ok: boolean; detail: string; source?: "JAVA_HOME" | "PATH" |
  null; java_home?: string };
    git_bash:{ ok: boolean; detail: string; path?: string };
    home_jdk:{ ok: boolean; detail: string; path: string | null }; // not
  provided home -> ok:false, detail "未提供 home 目录..."
  }
  ```

  Windows python Store alias detection: text containing "Microsoft Store"/"商店"
  counts as not installed (`env_check.rs:56-61`).

  ## 5. Sessions

  Handlers: `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/api/sessions.rs`.

  ### 5.1 GET `/api/sessions`
  `sessions.rs:4-35`

  ```ts
  // 200
  { sessions: Array<{
    id: string;
    assistant: string;
    cwd: string;
    model: string;
    messageCount: number;
    firstMessage: string;         // first message's content, or ""
    name: string | null;
    created: string;              // RFC3339
    modified: string;             // RFC3339
    isStreaming: boolean;
    agent_session_id: string | null;
  }> }
  ```

  Sorted by `modified` descending (lexicographic RFC3339). `isStreaming` also
  covers background adapt sessions, which stay marked for the whole run.

  ### 5.2 GET `/api/sessions/{id}`
  `sessions.rs:37-100`

  ```ts
  // 200
  {
    sessionId: string;            // note: "sessionId", not "id"
    assistant: string;
    cwd: string;
    model: string;
    name: string | null;
    messages: Message[];
    created: string;
    modified: string;
  }
  // 404
  { error: "Session not found" }
  ```

  Streaming behavior: if `streaming_state` exists, the handler mutates the
  **last** message if it is an assistant message, otherwise appends a new
  assistant message. Placeholder content is `"(streaming...)"` while
  `final_result` is empty. If `messages` is empty, the streaming state is not
  injected at all (`sessions.rs:46-83`).

  `agent_session_id` and `history_context` are not included here.

  ### 5.3 DELETE `/api/sessions/{id}`
  `sessions.rs:134-156`

  ```ts
  // 200
  { ok: true }
  // 404
  { error: "Session not found" }
  ```

  Also deletes the assistant-internal session. Does not delete the Claude jsonl
  file.

  ### 5.4 PATCH `/api/sessions/{id}/name`
  `sessions.rs:102-132`

  Request:
  ```ts
  { name?: string | null }  // omitted/null/"" clears the custom name
  ```
  ```ts
  // 200
  { ok: true }
  // 400
  { error: "会话名称不能超过 100 个字符" }   // chars(), trimmed
  // 404
  { error: "Session not found" }
  ```

  Name is trimmed before length check; empty restores the "show firstMessage"
  behavior.

  ## 6. Agent control and SSE

  All handlers in `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/api/agent.rs`.

  ### 6.1 POST `/api/agent/new`
  `agent.rs:275-363`

  Request (`NewSessionRequest`, `src/models.rs:55-64`):
  ```ts
  {
    cwd: string;                    // required, stored as-is (not validated to
  exist)
    message?: string;               // appended as a user message, but does NOT
  start streaming
    model?: string;
    assistant?: string;             // default "claude"
    resume_session_id?: string;     // Claude native sid to preload history /
  resume later
  }
  ```
  ```ts
  // 200
  { success: true; sessionId: string; assistant: string; model: string }
  // 400
  { success: false; error: "Assistant '<x>' not found" }
  // 500
  { success: false; error: string }
  ```

  Semantics:
  - Creates a cc-web session in memory + disk and a broadcast channel (capacity
  1024).
  - If `resume_session_id` is supplied, loads messages from
  `~/.claude/projects/*/<sid>.jsonl` for display and sets `agent_session_id` to
  that sid, so the next `/start` uses `--resume` instead of replaying
  auto-history.
  - Does not check whether the assistant CLI is actually installed.
  - Frontend usage: POST new, then open SSE, and only send `/start` after the
  SSE `connected` event (`static/app.js:1962-1993`).

  ### 6.2 POST `/api/agent/{id}/start`
  `agent.rs:365-629`

  Request:
  ```ts
  { message: string }
  ```
  ```ts
  // 200 (immediately; streaming happens in background)
  { success: true; sessionId: string }
  // 404
  { success: false; error: "Session not found" }
  // 409
  { success: false; error: "该会话正在执行中，请等待本次执行结束或先中断" }
  ```

  Semantics:
  - Concurrency guard: if the session is in `streaming_sessions`, rejects
  **before** appending the user message (409 leaves no trace).
  - Appends user message, saves to disk.
  - Ensures/reuses a broadcast channel, starts an event-saver task.
  - History injection: if there is no `history_context`, no `agent_session_id`,
  and `history_already_sent` is false and there are >1 messages, prepends a
  plain-text transcript (`"Here is the previous conversation history..."`) to
  the prompt. `history_context` is `take()`n and consumed.
  - If the assistant handle is missing at task start, it silently cleans
  streaming flags and the HTTP call has already returned 200.
  - Returns before the CLI process starts; errors arrive only through SSE as
  `error`.

  ### 6.3 POST `/api/agent/{id}/switch`
  `agent.rs:646-1011`

  Request:
  ```ts
  { assistant: string; model?: string }
  ```
  ```ts
  // 200, same assistant (no-op)
  { success: true; message: "Already using this assistant"; assistant: string }
  // 200, switched
  {
    success: true;
    sessionId: string;
    assistant: string;
    model: string;      // target assistant's DEFAULT model
    message: "Switched to <assistant>"
  }
  // 400
  { success: false; error: "Assistant '<x>' not found" }
  // 404
  { success: false; error: "Session not found" }
  // 500
  { success: false; error: string }
  ```

  Semantics and edge cases:
  - Builds a full plain-text history transcript, creates a fresh internal
  session for the new assistant, clears `agent_session_id`, sets
  `history_already_sent = true`, and appends a `system` message: `"🔄 Switched
  from X to Y. Conversation history preserved."`.
  - Immediately streams a context-acknowledgement prompt to the new assistant
  (one assistant turn happens without user input).
  - **`req.model` is effectively ignored for session metadata**: `session.model`
  and the response `model` are forced to `assistant.default_model()`; the
  requested model is only passed to `create_session`.
  - There is no concurrency guard here, unlike `/start`.

  ### 6.4 POST `/api/agent/{id}/abort`
  `agent.rs:1013-1075`

  No body.
  ```ts
  // 200 always
  { success: true; message: "Process killed" | "No running process found" }
  ```

  Semantics:
  - If the last message is a `user` message, it is popped (an aborted prompt is
  removed). If the last message is an assistant/partial message, it stays.
  - On Windows kills the process tree with `taskkill /F /T /PID <pid>`;
  otherwise SIGTERM.
  - Always removes the event channel, `streaming_sessions` entry and
  `streaming_state`, which closes any open SSE connection.
  - Returns 200 even for unknown sessions.

  ### 6.5 POST `/api/agent/{id}`
  `agent.rs:1077-1210`

  Request (`CommandRequest`, `src/models.rs:71-79`):
  ```ts
  {
    type: "save_message" | "set_model" | "get_state";
    message?: string;
    model?: string;
    content_blocks?: ContentBlock[];
  }
  ```
  Responses:
  ```ts
  // save_message / set_model success
  { success: true; data: null }
  // get_state success
  { success: true; data: {
    sessionId: string; assistant: string; model: string;
    messageCount: number; cwd: string; isStreaming: boolean;
  } }
  // validation / unknown command
  { success: false; error: "Message is required" | "Model is required" |
                            "Unknown command: <x>" | ... }   // 400
  // set_model assistant missing / internal failure
  { success: false; error: string }                        // 500
  // session missing
  { success: false; error: "Session not found" }           // 404
  ```

  `save_message` updates the last assistant message in place if the last message
  is an assistant message, otherwise appends a new assistant message.
  `set_model` updates both the assistant-internal session and `session.model`.

  ### 6.6 GET `/api/agent/{id}`
  `agent.rs:1212-1237`

  ```ts
  // 200, exists
  { running: true; state: {
    sessionId: string; assistant: string; model: string;
    messageCount: number; cwd: string; isStreaming: boolean;
  } }
  // 200, missing (never 404)
  { running: false }
  ```

  Frontend polls this (e.g. every 10 s) to detect stale streaming.

  ### 6.7 GET `/api/agent/{id}/events` (SSE)
  `agent.rs:1239-1329`; event producers `src/ai/streaming.rs:82-191`,
  `src/ai/{pi,codex}.rs`, `src/api/adapt_bg.rs:556,659,682`.

  - Not found: `404` with JSON `{ error: "Session not found" }`.
  - Otherwise: `200` with
    - `Content-Type: text/event-stream`
    - `Cache-Control: no-cache`
    - `Connection: keep-alive`
  - If no channel exists for the session, one is created.
  - SSE frames use only `data: <json>\n\n`; no `event:` name, no `id:` field, no
  replay/`Last-Event-ID`. The client uses `EventSource.onmessage`.
  - First frame is always:
  ```json
  {"type":"connected","sessionId":"<id>"}
  ```
  - Heartbeat every 15 s:
  ```json
  {"type":"heartbeat","sessionId":"<id>"}
  ```
  - Broadcast capacity 1024; lagged events are dropped and logged.
  - The stream ends when the broadcast channel is closed. `abort` deletes the
  channel; a normal `result` does **not** close it (the frontend closes the
  EventSource itself).
  - No backlog replay: late clients must call `GET /api/sessions/{id}` for
  accumulated content (the event saver persists progress continuously).

  Event union emitted on this channel:

  ```ts
  type AgentEvent =
    | { type: "connected"; sessionId: string }
    | { type: "heartbeat"; sessionId: string }
    | {
        type: "start";
        sessionId: string;          // cc-web session id
        model: string;
        agentSessionId?: string | null; // Claude only; Pi/Codex omit this key
      }
    | { type: "thinking"; thinking: string }
    | { type: "tool_call"; id: string; name: string; input: unknown } // input
  may be {} / null
    | { type: "tool_result"; id: string; output: string }             // id =
  tool_use id
    | { type: "chunk"; content: string }
    | { type: "result"; content: string; model: string }
    | { type: "error"; message: string }
    | { type: "adapt_paused" }                       // adapt only
    | { type: "adapt_finished"; status: string };    // adapt only:
  "done"|"aborted"|"failed"
  ```

  Per-producer details:
  - **Claude** (`ai/claude.rs:969-1040`): `start` includes `agentSessionId` from
  `system/init.session_id` (may be `null`). `result` maps `result` text +
  `is_error`; if `is_error`, emits `error` with `message`. If Claude exits
  without a terminal event, a fallback `error` is emitted (API error text or
  startup stderr).
  - **Pi** (`ai/pi.rs:612-815`): `start` has only `sessionId` + `model` (no
  `agentSessionId`). `thinking` is sent at `thinking_end`, `chunk` only for
  `text_delta`, `tool_call`/`tool_result` from tool events, final `result` at
  `agent_end` or fallback with accumulated text.
  - **Codex** (`ai/codex.rs:416-528`): `start` from `thread.started` (no
  `agentSessionId`); `chunk` from `message.delta`; `result` from
  `message.completed`/`turn.completed`; `tool_call` input is always `{}`; errors
  from `turn.failed`/`error` (reconnection messages suppressed).
  - **Adapt** (`api/adapt_bg.rs:556,659,682`): additionally emits `adapt_paused`
  when a patch finishes and the run waits for confirmation, and
  `adapt_finished` with `status` when the whole run ends. These are sent only if
  at least one SSE receiver exists.

  Saver semantics (`agent.rs:114-244`): `thinking` inserts before text blocks;
  `tool_call` de-duplicates by non-empty id; `tool_result` appends a block;
  `chunk` appends to the last text block; `result`/`error` finalize. Progress is
  written to session messages only once real text exists (`final_result`
  non-empty or a text block), otherwise only `streaming_state` is updated
  (`agent.rs:33-42`). Chunks are persisted at most about once per second.

  ### 6.8 GET `/api/claude-sessions/{sid}?full=1`
  `agent.rs:253-273`; parser `src/claude_history.rs:37-161`

  - `sid` must be non-empty and only `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`; otherwise 400 `{ error:
  "invalid session id" }`.
  - Locates `~/.claude/projects/*/<sid>.jsonl` (Windows `USERPROFILE`, else
  `HOME`).
  - Missing file returns `{ messages: [] }` with 200.

  ```ts
  // 200
  { messages: Message[] }   // Message shape from section 3
  ```

  `full=1`/`full=true` keeps `thinking` blocks; otherwise thinking is dropped.
  Sidechain and meta user events are skipped. Tool results are appended to the
  preceding assistant message's `content_blocks`. This endpoint is pure read: no
  session creation, no assistant locks, no disk writes.

  ---

  ## 7. Files

  Handlers: `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/api/files.rs`.

  ### 7.1 GET `/api/files?path=<dir>`
  `files.rs:188-273`

  - Default path `"."` (process working directory).
  - Missing path: 400 `{ success:false, error:"Path does not exist" }`.
  - Non-directory: 400 `{ success:false, error:"Path is not a directory" }`.
  - Read error: 500.
  - Hidden entries whose name starts with `.` are skipped.
  - Sort: directories first, then case-insensitive name.

  ```ts
  // 200
  {
    success: true;
    path: string;
    parent: string | null;
    files: Array<{
      name: string;
      path: string;     // absolute-ish path as produced by read_dir
      is_dir: boolean;
      size: number;     // 0 for directories
    }>;
  }
  ```

  ### 7.2 GET `/api/files/{path:.*}`
  `files.rs:275-326`

  Reads a text file via `fs::read_to_string`.

  ```ts
  // 200
  { success: true; path: string; content: string }
  // 404 { success:false, error:"File not found" }
  // 400 { success:false, error:"Path is a directory" }
  // 400 { success:false, error:"File too large (max 1MB)" }
  // 500 { success:false, error:"Failed to read file: ..." }  // includes
  non-UTF8
  ```

  ### 7.3 GET `/api/files/raw/{path:.*}`
  `files.rs:328-396`

  Returns raw bytes, no `Content-Disposition`. Used to open HTML/MD/TXT in a new
  tab.

  - Max 20 MB (400 `"File too large (max 20MB)"`).
  - 404 missing; 400 directory; 500 read failure (JSON).
  - Content-Type by extension: `html/htm` -> `text/html`, `css` -> `text/css`,
  `js` -> `application/javascript`,
  `md/txt/log/sql/java/xml/json/yaml/yml/properties/csv/bat/sh/cfg/ini` ->
  `text/plain`, images -> image MIME, `pdf` -> `application/pdf`, otherwise
  `application/octet-stream` (`files.rs:329-348`).

  ### 7.4 POST `/api/files/reveal`
  `files.rs:400-436`

  ```ts
  { path?: string }
  ```
  ```ts
  // 200 { success: true }
  // 400 { success:false, error:"File not found" }  // empty or non-existent
  // 500 { success:false, error:"Failed to open file manager: ..." }
  ```

  Runs `explorer /select,<path>` on Windows, `open -R` on macOS, `xdg-open
  <parent>` on Linux.

  ### 7.5 GET `/api/pick-folder?start=<dir>`
  `files.rs:73-186`

  Blocks until the user closes the native dialog; only one dialog at a time.

  ```ts
  // 200 chosen
  { success: true; path: string }
  // 200 cancelled
  { success: true; path: null }
  // 409 another dialog open
  { success: false; error: "已有目录选择窗口打开，请先处理它" }
  // 500 failure
  { success: false; error: string }
  ```

  `start` is used only if it exists and is a directory. Windows uses `rfd`;
  macOS uses `osascript choose folder`; Linux returns 500
  "该平台不支持图形目录选择".

  ### 7.6 implCon path whitelist
  `files.rs:17-71`

  In implCon, list/read/raw/reveal check that the canonical path is under at
  least one of:
  - `<exe dir>/temp` (all node workspaces)
  - `<exe dir>/recordings`
  - any session `cwd`
  - node run `out_dir`, `stage_dir`, `turn_dir`
  - node run `env_snapshot.package_path`, `.code_directory`, `.local_jdk_path`

  A disallowed existing path returns `403 { success:false,
  error:"实施版仅能访问..." }`. Non-existent paths pass through and later return
  404/400.

  ## 8. Attachments and node runs

  Handlers: `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/api/node_runs.rs`.

  ### 8.1 POST `/api/chat/attachment?session_id=<id>&name=<optional filename>`
  `node_runs.rs:247-272` (shared)

  - `multipart/form-data`; any file field names; non-file fields are discarded.
  - `session_id` must exist and have a non-empty `cwd`; else 404.
  - Target dir: `<session.cwd>/.cc-web-attachments/`.
  - Filename sanitization: Unicode alphanumerics plus `- _ . ( )` kept, others
  -> `_`; empty -> `attachment.bin`.
  - Stored as `<uuid-simple>_<safe-name>`.
  - Per-file limit 20 MB; blocked extensions `exe,bat,cmd,com,msi,scr,dll,ps1`
  (case-insensitive).
  - If no file field is present: 400 `"没有收到文件"`.

  ```ts
  // 200
  { success: true; path: string; paths: string[] } // path = first saved file,
  paths = all
  // 400/404/500
  { success: false; error: string }
  ```

  ### 8.2 GET `/api/node/runs`
  `node_runs.rs:53-64`

  ```ts
  // 200
  { code: 0; data: NodeRun[]; host: string } // host = COMPUTERNAME/HOSTNAME or
  "unknown"
  ```

  `data` order is not guaranteed. Run objects are opaque JSON defined by the
  frontend. Known fields used by the current frontend (`static/node_core.js`):
  `id`, `phase` (`analyzing|merging|awaiting_decision|done|failed`), `verdict`,
  `problem_desc`, `stage_dir`, `out_dir`, `turn_dir`, `step_dir`, `stage`,
  `phase1_step`, `session_id`, `agent_session_id` (Claude sid), `reported`,
  `updated_at`.

  ### 8.3 PUT `/api/node/runs/{id}`
  `node_runs.rs:66-83`

  - Body must be a JSON object; otherwise 400 `{ error: "运行记录必须是 JSON
  对象" }`.
  - `id.len() > 128` -> 400 `{ error:"运行标识过长" }`.
  - The body's `id` is overwritten with the path id.
  - Whole-object upsert, no field merge. Writes
  `~/.cc-web(-novice)/node_runs.json` asynchronously.

  ```ts
  // 200
  { code: 0; data: null }
  ```

  ### 8.4 DELETE `/api/node/runs/{id}`
  `node_runs.rs:85-95`

  ```ts
  // 200
  { code: 0; data: { deleted: 0 | 1 } }
  ```

  Does not delete workspace files or the associated cc-web session.

  ### 8.5 POST `/api/node/workspace`
  `node_runs.rs:97-128` (shared; mainly implCon)

  ```ts
  { run_id?: string }
  ```
  - `run_id` must match `[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}`; creates `<exe
  dir>/temp/node/<run_id>/` idempotently.

  ```ts
  // 200
  { success: true; path: string }
  // 400
  { success: false; error: "run_id 只允许字母、数字、- 和 _（长度 1~128）" }
  // 500
  { success: false; error: string }
  ```

  ### 8.6 POST `/api/node/problem-image?run_id=<id>&name=<optional>` (implCon
  only)
  `node_runs.rs:220-241`

  Multipart, same file rules as chat attachment. Target: `<exe
  dir>/temp/node/<run_id>/images/`. Same response/error shapes as 8.1. Rejected
  in dev build (404 plain text).

  ## 9. Update / version

  Handlers: `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/api/update.rs`.

  ### 9.1 POST `/api/update/check`
  `update.rs:164-213`

  ```ts
  { server_url: string; auth_token: string }
  ```
  ```ts
  // 200 normal
  { success: true; data: {
    update_available: boolean;
    local: LocalMeta;
    remote: RemoteMeta | null;   // passthrough of patch_search "data"
  } }
  // 200 shell-managed build (CC_WEB_SHELL=1 or CC_WEB_DISABLE_UPDATE=1)
  { success: true; data: {
    shell_managed: true;
    update_available: false;
    local: LocalMeta | null;
  } }
  // 400
  { success: false; error: "缺少补丁中心地址或凭据" }
  // 200 for remote/network failure
  { success: false; error: string }
  // 500 for spawn/join failure
  { success: false; error: string }
  ```

  `LocalMeta` = `data` from `/api/version` (section 4.1). `RemoteMeta` fields
  consumed by the frontend: `version`, `build_unix`, `has_package`, `sha256`,
  `size`; others are server-defined.

  `update_available` is true only when `remote != null`, `remote.has_package !==
  false`, and `remote.version !== local.version` (`update.rs:191-204`).

  ### 9.2 POST `/api/update/apply`
  `update.rs:499-648`

  Same request body as check. Response is a **plain-text line stream**, not SSE
  and not JSON:

  - HTTP 400: missing `server_url`/`auth_token` (JSON).
  - HTTP 400: shell-managed build (JSON `"桌面版不支持单独更新 cc-web..."`).
  - HTTP 409: another update already running (JSON `"更新正在进行中"`).
  - Otherwise 200 with `Content-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8`,
  `Cache-Control: no-cache`, one line per event:
  ```
  progress <downloadedBytes> <totalBytes>   // every 256 KB; total may be 0 if
  server sent no Content-Length
  ok <json>                                 // success; process exits shortly
  afterwards on Windows / restart path
  error <message>                           // newlines replaced with spaces
  ```
  `ok` payloads:
  ```ts
  // already latest
  { success: true; message: "本机已经是最新版本（<v>），无需更新" }
  // Windows / restart path
  { success: true; message: "新版本已下载并校验通过，cc-web 正在重启" }
  // macOS with restart env
  { success: true; replaced: true; restarted: true; message: "..." }
  // macOS without restart env
  { success: true; replaced: true; restarted: false; message:
  "新版本已就位，重启 cc-web 后生效" }
  ```
  The download URL used internally is
  `<server>/api/ccweb/download/<version>/<platform>?edition=<dev|novice>` with
  `Authorization: Bearer <auth_token>`.

  ---

  ## 10. Adapt (dev-only)

  Handlers: `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/api/adapt.rs`;
  background engine and types: `src/api/adapt_bg.rs`.

  ### 10.1 AdaptRun / AdaptItem types
  `adapt_bg.rs:16-54`

  ```ts
  type AdaptRunStatus = "running" | "awaiting_confirmation" | "done" | "failed"
  | "aborted";
  type AdaptItemStatus = "pending" | "running" | "done" | "conflict" |
  "nosource" | "failed" | "skipped";

  interface AdaptRun {
    id: string;
    project_dir: string;
    product_name: string;
    product_version: string;
    session_id: string;             // cc-web session id
    agent_session_id: string;       // Claude native sid, "" until known
    items: AdaptItem[];
    status: AdaptRunStatus;
    report: string | null;
    started_at: string;             // RFC3339 millis
    finished_at: string | null;
    server_url: string;
    auth_token: string;             // WARNING: serialized to clients
    abort_requested: boolean;
    auto_continue: boolean;
  }

  interface AdaptItem {
    seq: number;
    patch_name: string;
    problem_desc: string;
    dir: string;
    status: AdaptItemStatus;
    changed_files: string | null;
    conflict_detail: string | null;
    note: string | null;
    started_at: string | null;
    finished_at: string | null;
    output_conclusion: string | null; // 结论.md
    output_summary: string | null;    // 摘要.md
  }
  ```

  Note: `AdaptRun` serializes `auth_token` in list/get responses. Treat this as
  a security edge case to remove in the rewrite.

  ### 10.2 POST `/api/adapt/upload?run_id=<id>&seq=<n>`
  `adapt.rs:68-127`

  - `multipart/form-data`, any file field name.
  - `run_id` is sanitized (non `[A-Za-z0-9._-]` -> `_`); empty after sanitize ->
  400.
  - Writes each uploaded file to `<exe
  dir>/temp/adapt/<run_id>/<seq>/<sanitized-name>`.
  - Extracts ZIP entries (path traversal blocked via `enclosed_name`) into the
  same dir.
  - If multiple files are uploaded, all are written; `zip`/`entries` describe
  the last one.

  ```ts
  // 200 extraction ok
  { success: true; dir: string; zip: string; entries: number }
  // 200 extraction failed (HTTP 200!)
  { success: false; error: "解压失败：..."; zip: string }
  // 400 no file / body read error / empty run_id
  { success: false; error: string }
  // 500 directory/write failure
  { success: false; error: string }
  ```

  ### 10.3 POST `/api/adapt/import`
  `adapt.rs:129-222`

  ```ts
  {
    run_id: string;
    seq: number;
    patch_id: string;
    patch_name?: string;   // fallback filename if Content-Disposition missing
    server_url: string;
    auth_token: string;
  }
  ```
  Streams `text/plain` lines:
  ```
  progress <downloaded> <total>   // every 64 KB; total 0 if unknown
  extracting
  ok <json>                       // {success:true,dir,zip,name,entries}
  error <message>
  ```
  Pre-stream validation errors are normal JSON: 400 empty
  run_id/patch_id/missing creds; 500 cannot locate exe dir/create dir.

  Internal download: `GET <server>/api/patches/<patch_id>/download` with Bearer
  token (`adapt_bg.rs:725-796`). 401/403 -> "补丁中心凭据失效..."; 404 ->
  "补丁文件在服务器上不存在...".

  ### 10.4 GET `/api/adapt/git-status?path=<dir>`
  `adapt.rs:224-262`

  ```ts
  // 200
  { success: true; is_git: boolean; has_changes: boolean; summary?: string;
  reason?: string }
  // 400
  { success: false; error: "path 不能为空" }
  ```
  Missing/non-directory path -> 200
  `{success:true,is_git:false,has_changes:false,reason:"目录不存在"}`. No `.git`
  -> `is_git:false`. `git status --porcelain` failure ->
  `is_git:true,has_changes:false,reason:"git 命令不可用"`. `summary` is the
  first 20 porcelain lines.

  ### 10.5 POST `/api/adapt/start`
  `adapt.rs:269-436`

  ```ts
  {
    project_dir: string;
    product_name?: string;
    product_version?: string;
    server_url: string;
    auth_token: string;
    items: Array<{
      seq: number;
      patch_name: string;
      problem_desc: string;
      dir: string;        // extracted patch dir
    }>;
  }
  ```
  ```ts
  // 200
  { success: true; run_id: string; message: "适配已在后台执行，可关闭页面。..."
  }
  // 400
  { success: false; error: "补丁列表为空" | "客开工程目录为空" | "缺少
  patch_search token" }
  // 409
  { success: false; error: "已有适配任务正在执行，请等它跑完或先中止" }
  // 500
  { success: false; error: "ClaudeCode 不可用" | "创建会话失败：..." }
  ```

  Creates a real Claude session plus a cc-web session (`assistant:"claude"`,
  `cwd=project_dir`) and an event channel, so the run can be viewed in the chat
  UI. All items start as `pending`; run starts as `running`. Background work
  happens in a dedicated thread using a single-thread Tokio runtime
  (`adapt_bg.rs:369-381`). The session is kept in `streaming_sessions` for the
  whole run, which keeps `isStreaming=true` and blocks `/start`.

  ### 10.6 GET `/api/adapt/runs`
  `adapt.rs:438-443`

  ```ts
  // 200
  { success: true; data: AdaptRun[] }   // HashMap order, unsorted
  ```

  ### 10.7 GET `/api/adapt/runs/{id}`
  `adapt.rs:445-453`

  ```ts
  // 200 { success: true; data: AdaptRun }
  // 404 { success: false; error: "适配任务不存在" }
  ```

  ### 10.8 POST `/api/adapt/runs/{id}/abort`
  `adapt.rs:674-717`

  No body.
  - Status `running`: sets `abort_requested=true`; returns 200
  `{success:true,message:"中止请求已提交..."}`. Current patch finishes;
  remaining are skipped.
  - Status `awaiting_confirmation`: marks remaining skipped and finalizes as
  `aborted` immediately.
  - Other status: 400 `{ success:false, error:"该任务不在执行中" }`.
  - Missing: 404.

  ### 10.9 POST `/api/adapt/runs/{id}/next` and `/next-all`
  `adapt.rs:455-534`

  Optional body (the whole JSON may be absent):
  ```ts
  { server_url: string; auth_token: string }  // refreshes stored credentials
  ```
  - 409 if any adapt run is `running`.
  - 404 missing run.
  - 400 if the run is not `awaiting_confirmation`.
  - 200 `{ success:true, message:string }`.
  - `next` runs one more patch then pauses again; `next-all` sets
  `auto_continue=true` and runs to the end without pausing.

  ### 10.10 POST `/api/adapt/runs/{id}/retry-failed`
  `adapt.rs:536-599`

  No body.
  - 409 if any run is running.
  - 404 missing.
  - 400 `{success:false,error:"没有失败的补丁可重试"}` if nothing moved.
  - 200 `{success:true,message:"已把 N 个失败的补丁放回队列..."}`.
  - Moves every `failed` item back to `pending`, clears
  note/changed_files/conflict_detail/outputs/timestamps. If any moved and run is
  not `awaiting_confirmation`, sets run to `awaiting_confirmation` and clears
  `finished_at`/`report`. Does not auto-start.

  ### 10.11 POST `/api/adapt/runs/{id}/delete`
  `adapt.rs:601-653`

  Optional body `{ server_url, auth_token }` used to delete the server ledger
  entry (otherwise stored creds).

  ```ts
  // 200
  { success: true; server_deleted: boolean; message: string }
  // 404 { success:false, error:"适配任务不存在" }
  // 409 { success:false, error:"该任务正在执行中，请先中止再删除" }
  ```
  Deletes the local record and attempts `DELETE
  <server>/api/patch-adapt/runs/<local_run_id>`. Does not delete extracted patch
  files or the cc-web session.

  ### 10.12 POST `/api/adapt/runs/{id}/re-report`
  `adapt.rs:655-672`

  Required body `{ server_url, auth_token }`. Refreshes stored creds and pushes
  current state to the server.

  ```ts
  // 200 { success: true }
  // 404 { success:false, error:"适配任务不存在（本机没有这条记录）" }
  // 400 if body missing/invalid (actix extractor)
  ```
  Server reporting failures are swallowed; `success:true` only guarantees the
  local record exists and a report was attempted.

  ### 10.13 Outbound adapt reports (not cc-web routes)
  `adapt_bg.rs:295-365, 698-723`
  - `POST {server}/api/patch-adapt/runs` body:
  ```ts
  {
    local_run_id, product_name: string|null, product_version: string|null,
    code_directory, status, patch_count, succeeded_count,
    conflict_count, nosource_count, failed_count,
    client_host, report, started_at, finished_at
  }
  ```
  - `POST {server}/api/patch-adapt/runs/{local_run_id}/items` body `{ items:
  Array<item fields without dir> }`.
  - `DELETE {server}/api/patch-adapt/runs/{local_run_id}`.
  All use `Authorization: Bearer <auth_token>`, 15 s timeout; failures are only
  logged.

  ## 11. Local Claude (dev-only)

  Handler: `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/api/local_claude.rs`.

  ### 11.1 POST `/api/local-claude/execute`
  `local_claude.rs:80-176`

  ```ts
  {
    execution_id: string;
    system_prompt: string;   // <= 200_000 bytes
    user_prompt: string;     // <= 200_000 bytes
    cwd?: string;            // must be an absolute existing directory
    model?: string;
  }
  ```
  ```ts
  // 200, even when the run itself failed
  {
    execution_id: string;
    text: string | null;
    error: string | null;
    session_id: string | null;     // Claude native session id
    conclusion: string | null;     // contents of <cwd>/结论.md
    summary: string | null;        // contents of <cwd>/摘要.md
  }
  // 400
  { success: false; error: "invalid request" | "working directory is required" |
                            "working directory must be an absolute path" |
                            "working directory does not exist" |
                            "working directory is not a directory" |
                            "working directory is not valid UTF-8" }
  // 409
  { success: false; error: "execution_id was already used for a different
  request" }
  ```

  Idempotency: same `execution_id` + identical fingerprint (`system_prompt`,
  `user_prompt`, `cwd`, `model`) reuses the in-flight result; a different
  fingerprint returns 409. Results stay in an in-memory map indefinitely (no
  cleanup). On success it also copies `结论.md`/`摘要.md` to `<exe
  dir>/temp/<execution_id>_结论.md` and `_摘要.md`.

  ### 11.2 POST `/api/local-claude/{execution_id}/cancel`
  `local_claude.rs:178-180`

  Always 501:
  ```ts
  { success: false, error: "cancellation is not implemented for this execution"
  }
  ```

  ## 12. Recorder (implCon-only, CDP)

  Handler: `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/recorder.rs`. Not
  compiled in dev; in dev the three routes 404 plain text.

  ### 12.1 POST `/api/recorder/open`
  `recorder.rs:1281-1381`

  ```ts
  {
    record_url?: string;   // preferred; must start with http:// or https://
    env_id?: number;       // legacy fallback: look up record_url from
  patch_search
  }
  ```
  - If `record_url` is present, validate it.
  - Else if `env_id` is present, requires an `Authorization` header. cc-web
  calls `GET <patch_search_server>/api/project-envs` with the same header and
  finds `id == env_id`, then uses its `record_url`.
  - Else 400 `"缺少「录制页面地址」"`.

  ```ts
  // 200
  { success: true; data: null }
  // 400
  { success: false; error: "缺少「录制页面地址」" | "「录制页面地址」必须是
  http/https 地址" |
                            "缺少登录凭据（Authorization 头）" |
  "找不到该产品环境..." | ... }
  // 500
  { success: false; error: string }   // browser launch/CDP failure
  ```

  Actual behavior: `launch()` first calls `close_browser()`, so opening while
  already open **restarts** the recording browser and clears prior records
  (`recorder.rs:302-314`). Browser profile: `<exe dir>/temp/recorder-profile`.
  The user starts/stops recording from a control injected into the browser page;
  the HTTP API only opens, polls and closes.

  ### 12.2 GET `/api/recorder/status`
  `recorder.rs:1383-1423`

  ```ts
  // 200
  {
    success: true;
    state: "idle" | "browser_open" | "recording" | "finished";
    file: string | null;          // exported JSON absolute path
    interfaces: Array<{
      method: string;
      url: string;
      status: number | null;
      failed: string;             // "" if not failed
    }>;                            // capped at first 300 records
    error: string;                 // last launch/CDP error, "" normally
  }
  ```
  If the user closes the browser without stopping recording, the status handler
  detects child exit and resets to `idle` (no export). `finished` means an
  export file exists.

  ### 12.3 POST `/api/recorder/close`
  `recorder.rs:1425-1438`

  No body. If recording, flushes in-flight bodies, exports and closes; otherwise
  just closes the browser.

  ```ts
  // 200
  { success: true; data: null }
  ```

  Export file: `<exe dir>/recordings/record_<YYYYMMDDHHMMSS>.json`
  (`recorder.rs:641-732`), shape:
  ```ts
  {
    exportedAt: number; startedAt: number; pageUrl: string;
    interfaces: Array<{
      id: number; time: number; method: string; url: string; type: string;
      status: number | null; duration: number; size: number; mime: string;
      failed: string; error?: string; pageUrl: string;
      requestHeaders: Record<string, string>;
      requestBody: string; responseBody: string; bodyTruncated: boolean;
    }>;
  }
  ```
  Sensitive headers/body fields are masked (`recorder.rs:1103-1247`).

  ## 13. Static pages, `/login`, and auth/token behavior

  ### 13.1 Static routes
  `D:/project/AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop/src/static_files.rs:81-144`. All
  responses include `ETag`, `Cache-Control: no-cache, must-revalidate`;
  `If-None-Match` yields 304.

  | Path | Dev build | implCon build |
  |---|---|---|
  | `/`, `/patches.html` | `patches.html` | `novice.html` |
  | `/chat.html`, `/index.html` | `index.html` | `novice.html` |
  | `/login`, `/login.html` | **404** (not routed) | `novice_login.html` |
  | `/novice.html` | 404 | `novice.html` |
  | `/app.js`, `/style.css`, `/common.js`, `/folderpick.js`, `/node_core.js`,
  `/prompts_novice.js`, `/chat_attachments.js` | served | served |
  | `/patches.js`, `/sidenav.js`, `/copybox.js`, `/workflow_run.html`,
  `/node_run.html`, `/adapt_run.html`, `/server_replay.html` | served | 404 |
  | `/novice.js`, `/novice_chat.js`, `/novice_login.js` | only `novice_chat.js`
  | all served |

  ### 13.2 There is no cc-web login endpoint
  The frontend's login flow talks **directly to patch_search**, not to cc-web:

  - `POST <patch_search_server>/api/auth/login` body `{ username, password }` ->
  frontend expects `{ code: 0, data: { access_token: string, user: object } }`
  (`static/common.js:135-170`).
  - `GET <patch_search_server>/api/auth/me` -> `{ code:0, data: user }`
  (`common.js:172-194`).
  - `POST /api/auth/logout` (`static/novice.js:111`), `GET /api/auth/profile`,
  `POST /api/auth/change-password`.
  - `patchRequest()` requires `payload.code === 0`, rotates across
  `patch_search_servers`, and treats 401 (except on login) as session expiry
  (`common.js:90-133`).

  Token storage: `localStorage["patch-search-access-token"]`. It is sent as
  `Authorization: Bearer <token>` to patch_search. There are **no cookies, no
  CSRF tokens, and no server-side auth state in cc-web**.

  ### 13.3 Where cc-web consumes credentials
  - `POST /api/recorder/open` with `env_id`: forwards the incoming
  `Authorization` header to patch_search.
  - `POST /api/update/check`, `/api/update/apply`, `/api/adapt/start`,
  `/api/adapt/import`, `/api/adapt/runs/{id}/next*`, `/re-report`, `/delete`:
  credentials are passed in the **JSON body** as `server_url` + `auth_token`,
  then used as `Authorization: Bearer` outbound.
  - All other `/api/*` routes need no credentials. In implCon there is no CORS
  header, so cross-origin browser scripts cannot read responses even though LAN
  clients can reach the port.

  ---

  ## 14. Frontend-facing sequencing and important edge cases

  1. **New chat flow**: `POST /api/agent/new` -> `GET /api/agent/{id}/events`
  (EventSource) -> on `connected` -> `POST /api/agent/{id}/start`
  (`static/app.js:1431-1434, 1962-1993`). Do not call `/start` before opening
  SSE, or early events are lost; late events are recoverable from `GET
  /api/sessions/{id}`.
  2. **`result` does not close the server channel**. The frontend closes the
  EventSource. For adapt sessions, `result` is per-patch; only
  `adapt_paused`/`adapt_finished` end the turn (`static/app.js:1519-1554`).
  3. **`/start` returns 200 before the CLI runs**. All execution failures are
  delivered as SSE `error`; the HTTP response only reports
  validation/concurrency problems.
  4. **Abort is best-effort and always 200**. It removes the last user message,
  kills the process tree, and deletes the channel. If the channel is deleted
  while SSE is open, the SSE loop ends.
  5. **`switch` silently ignores the requested `model`** in session metadata and
  performs an extra assistant turn for context acknowledgement.
  6. **`new` does not validate `cwd`**. The assistant is later launched with
  that cwd; invalid paths fail at stream time.
  7. **Session detail injection only works when `messages` is non-empty**. An
  empty session with active streaming state returns an empty `messages` array.
  8. **Mixed naming**: `sessionId` in agent/session-detail responses;
  `session_id` in adapt/node objects; `agent_session_id` in session list;
  `agentSessionId` inside SSE `start`.
  9. **`AdaptRun.auth_token` is returned to the client**. Remove it in the
  TypeScript model or redact server-side in the rewrite.
  10. **Adapt background sessions are "streaming" for the whole run**, so
  `/api/sessions` shows `isStreaming=true` and `/start` is blocked even between
  patches.
  11. **Unimplemented feature routes return 404 plain text**, not JSON, because
  the static fallback handles them. A typed client should branch on
  `response.ok` before `response.json()`.
  12. **SSE heartbeat is 15 s**; implementations should consider a connection
  stale after roughly 2-3 missed heartbeats. The current client also uses `GET
  /api/agent/{id}` polling as a secondary liveness check.
  13. **Event saver is the only persistence path for partial output**; it only
  writes to `session.messages` once real text exists. Purely thinking/tool-only
  failed runs leave no assistant message on disk, only transient
  `streaming_state`.
  14. **`/api/debug/state` and `/api/patch-config` have no success envelope**,
  unlike most other endpoints.
  15. **Node run put uses `{code,data}` while every other cc-web write uses
  `{success}`**, so normalize in the API client layer.
  16. **implCon file access is whitelisted**; the same path may work in dev and
  403 in implCon.

  # CC-Web Chat Frontend — Behavioral Specification (for a React/TypeScript
  rewrite)

  Base directory for all paths below: `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop`

  ## 0. Scope, entry points, editions

  ### 0.1 Files and load order

  The chat page is `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop\static\index.html`.
  Scripts load in this exact order (`index.html:216-223`):

  1. `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop\static\folderpick.js`
  2. `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop\static\common.js`
  3. `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop\static\node_core.js`
  4. `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop\static\prompts_novice.js`
  5. `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop\static\app.js` (the entire chat
  implementation)
  6. `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop\static\novice_chat.js`
  7. `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop\static\chat_attachments.js`

  `static\sidenav.js` and `static\copybox.js` are **not loaded by the chat
  page**; they belong to the patch-center pages (`patches.html`,
  `node_run.html`, `adapt_run.html`, `workflow_run.html`). They are summarized
  in Appendix A because they were in scope, but they do not affect chat
  behavior.

  `static\style.css` supplies the layout and theme tokens.

  ### 0.2 Edition routing (important for rewrite scope)

  `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop\src\static_files.rs:105-128`
  (development edition):

  - `/` and `/patches.html` serve the patch-center SPA.
  - `/chat.html` and `/index.html` serve `index.html` (the page specified here).

  `src\static_files.rs:84-103` (`implCon` / “novice” edition):

  - `/`, `/chat.html`, `/index.html`, `/novice.html` all serve `novice.html`.
  - `app.js` is still served and reused, but `novice.html` hides the topbar and
  file browser (`novice.html:201-211`, `172-177`) and adds a different
  shell/menu.
  - `novice_chat.js` activates only when `/api/version` reports `data.edition
  === "novice"` (`novice_chat.js:542-571`). On the development chat page it
  detects `dev` and returns without doing anything.

  # Frontend behavior spec — AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop

  Anchor convention: every `file:line` below is under
  `D:\project\AgentHub-web-shishi-desktop\`. The target files themselves are
  absolute in each section heading.

  ---

  ## 0. Build/routing context (critical before rewriting)

  Two mutually exclusive builds share the same `static/` tree.

  | Build | Pages served | Router source |
  |---|---|---|
  | `implCon` ("novice"/desktop build) | `/`, `/chat.html`, `/index.html`,
  `/novice.html` → `novice.html`; `/login`, `/login.html` → `novice_login.html`;
  assets `novice.js`, `novice_chat.js`, `novice_login.js`, `common.js`,
  `app.js`, `node_core.js`, `prompts_novice.js`, `folderpick.js`,
  `chat_attachments.js` | `src/static_files.rs:84-103` |
  | dev build | `/`, `/patches.html` → `patches.html`; `/chat.html`,
  `/index.html` → `index.html`; `workflow_run.html`, `node_run.html`,
  `adapt_run.html`, `server_replay.html`; `sidenav.js`, `copybox.js` |
  `src/static_files.rs:105-128` |

  Consequences:

  - `workflow_run.html`, `node_run.html`, `server_replay.html` are **not
  reachable in the novice/desktop build**; they are dev-build-only. Conversely
  `novice.html`, `novice_login.html/js` are not reachable in dev.
  - The novice shell is a full-page app; `sidenav.js` is not loaded there. In
  the dev build, `sidenav.js` has a novice menu branch
  (`static/sidenav.js:63-99`) whose `/novice.html?view=env|check` links are dead
  because dev does not serve `novice.html`.
  - No CORS middleware is installed in `implCon` (`src/main.rs:348-360`); all
  pages are same-origin with cc-web.

  ### Two backends

  | Backend | Base | Auth | Response convention |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | cc-web (same origin) | `''` | none | Mostly `{success:true,...}` or
  `{code:0,data:...}`; some endpoints return bare objects (`/api/patch-config`,
  `/api/version`, `/api/sessions`, `/api/agent/*`) |
  ...
  </persisted-output>

      371 +/** 直接向 patch_search 登录（cc-web 本身没有登录接口）。 */  
      372 +export async function patchLogin(                             
      373 +  username: string,                                           
      374 +  password: string                                            
      375 +): Promise<{ token: string; user: PatchUser }> {              
      376 +  const { servers } = await getPatchServers();                
      377 +  if (!servers.length) throw new ApiError('补丁中心地址未配置 
          +，请联系管理员');                                             
      378 +  let lastError: unknown;                                     
      379 +  for (const base of servers) {                               
      380 +    try {                                                     
      381 +      const response = await fetch(`${base.replace(/\/+$/, '')
          +}/api/auth/login`, {                                          
      382 +        method: 'POST',                                       
      383 +        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },      
      384 +        body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }),         
      385 +      });                                                     
      386 +      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null);
      387 +      if (!response.ok || !payload || payload.code !== 0) {   
      388 +        throw new ApiError(                                   
      389 +          payload?.message || payload?.detail || (response.sta
          +tus === 401 ? '用户名或密码错误' : `登录失败（HTTP ${response.
          +status}）`),                                                  
      390 +          response.status                                     
      391 +        );                                                    
      392 +      }                                                       
      393 +      const data = payload.data || {};                        
      394 +      if (!data.access_token) throw new ApiError('登录响应缺少
          + token');                                                     
      395 +      try {                                                   
      396 +        localStorage.setItem('patch-search-access-token', data
          +.access_token);                                               
      397 +      } catch {                                               
      398 +        /* ignore */                                          
      399 +      }                                                       
      400 +      clearPatchServerCache();                                
      401 +      return { token: data.access_token, user: data.user || {}
          + };                                                           
      402 +    } catch (error) {                                         
      403 +      lastError = error;                                      
      404 +      if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status) break;   
      405 +    }                                                         
      406 +  }                                                           
      407 +  throw lastError instanceof Error ? lastError : new ApiError(
          +'无法连接补丁中心');                                          
      408 +}                                                             
      409 +                                                              
      410 +export async function patchMe(token?: string): Promise<PatchUs
          +er | null> {                                                  
      411 +  const auth = token ?? (() => {                              
      412 +    try {                                                     
      413 +      return localStorage.getItem('patch-search-access-token')
          + || '';                                                       
      414 +    } catch {                                                 
      415 +      return '';                                              
      416 +    }                                                         
      417 +  })();                                                       
      418 +  if (!auth) return null;                                     
      419 +  const { servers } = await getPatchServers();                
      420 +  let lastError: unknown;                                     
      421 +  for (const base of servers) {                               
      422 +    try {                                                     
      423 +      const response = await fetch(`${base.replace(/\/+$/, '')
          +}/api/auth/me`, {                                             
      424 +        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${auth}` },         
      425 +      });                                                     
      426 +      if (response.status === 401) {                          
      427 +        try {                                                 
      428 +          localStorage.removeItem('patch-search-access-token')
          +;                                                             
      429 +        } catch {                                             
      430 +          /* ignore */                                        
      431 +        }                                                     
      432 +        return null;                                          
      433 +      }                                                       
      434 +      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null);
      435 +      if (!response.ok || !payload || payload.code !== 0) {   
      436 +        throw new ApiError(payload?.message || `HTTP ${respons
          +e.status}`, response.status);                                 
      437 +      }                                                       
      438 +      return (payload.data || {}) as PatchUser;               
      439 +    } catch (error) {                                         
      440 +      lastError = error;                                      
      441 +    }                                                         
      442 +  }                                                           
      443 +  if (lastError) throw lastError;                             
      444 +  return null;                                                
      445 +}

       41 +function AuthGuard({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {   
       42 +  const edition = useAppStore((state) => state.edition);      
       43 +  const location = useLocation();                             
       44 +  if (edition !== 'novice') return <>{children}</>;           
       45 +  let token = '';                                             
       46 +  try {                                                       
       47 +    token = localStorage.getItem('patch-search-access-token') 
          +|| '';                                                        
       48 +  } catch {                                                   
       49 +    /* ignore */                                              
       50 +  }                                                           
       51 +  if (!token) {                                               
       52 +    return <Navigate to={`/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(loc
          +ation.pathname)}`} replace />;                                
       53 +  }                                                           
       54 +  return <>{children}</>;                                     
       55 +}

          +estInit = {}): Promise<T> {

● React 重构已完成，并且已经把新前端重新编进两个 exe。下面是交付说明。

  已完成

  技术栈：Vite 4 + React 18 + TypeScript 5 + Tailwind 3 + Zustand（本机 Node
  14，所以锁定 Vite 4 兼容版本）。

  新的前端目录：frontend/，56 个源码文件、约 15,400 行 TS/TSX。

  页面覆盖

  ┌───────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ 页面  │                              状态                               │
  ├───────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 聊天  │                                                                 │
  │ 页    │ 会话列表、SSE 流式、思考/工具调用/工具结果折叠块、Markdown、消  │
  │ /chat │ 息队列、中止、附件粘贴上传、Claude 历史只读回放、文件浏览/预览  │

  后端与构建接入

  - src/static_files.rs 改为服务 SPA：所有页面路由返回同一个 React
    外壳，/app.js、/app.css 固定文件名内嵌。
  - build.bat 和 GitHub Actions 都改为先构建前端，再 cargo build。
  - static/dist/ 已生成并提交在工作区，Rust 可直接编译。
  - 已重新构建 cc-web-dev.exe 与 cc-web-implCon.exe。
  - 版本差异仍由 /api/version 的 edition 在运行时判断，不再分两套 HTML。

  验证结果

  - npx tsc --noEmit 通过
  - npm run build 通过（app.js 538KB / gzip 159KB，app.css 34.5KB）
  - cargo check、cargo check --features implCon 通过
  - cargo build --release 两版均通过
  - 用无头 Edge
    实际截图验证了：聊天会话渲染、补丁中心登录门、实施版外壳、环境检查、登录页
  - 最终 release 版路由全部返回
    200：/、/chat.html、/login、三个运行详情页、/app.js、/app.css

  使用方式

  # 直接运行（已重新打包）
  cc-web-dev.exe        # 3030 开发版
  cc-web-implCon.exe    # 3031 实施版

  # 前端开发
  cd frontend && npm run dev
  # 调实施版接口时：
  VITE_PROXY_TARGET=http://127.0.0.1:3031 npm run dev

  # 完整重新构建
  build.bat

  需要知道的简化点

  - 补丁中心深度后台功能做了可用的精简版：列宽拖拽、节点 timing
    明细、服务端影子记录合并/修复、会话归档上报等没有逐项复刻。
  - 智能开发提示词优先使用补丁中心配置的模板；未配置时使用内置精简模板，不是旧版
    那份超长文案。
  - 分屏面板位置记忆、面板级独立模型选择做了简化。
  - 旧的 static/*.html、app.js、patches.js
    仍保留在仓库用于对照，但已不被服务；tests/frontend/*_smoke.js 针对旧
    DOM，已新增说明文档，不再适用于新前端。
  - 单文件 bundle 是为了配合 Rust include_str!；后续若要拆包，需要让
    static_files.rs 支持 assets/ 目录。

  代码尚未提交，你可以先运行两个 exe 看效果，确认后再决定是否提交。

● 已按要求去掉模型选择功能，开发版/实施版共用同一套聊天组件，所以两边同时生效。

  改动内容

  - 聊天顶部栏：删除“模型”下拉框，只保留“助手”选择（Claude Code 等）。
  - 新建会话弹窗：删除模型下拉框，只选择工作目录和助手。
  - 前端不再请求 /api/models，也不再在新建会话/切换助手时传 model。
  - 后端行为不变：未指定模型时自动使用对应助手的默认模型（Claude/Pi/Codex
    均如此）。
  - 分屏面板、实施版统一外壳、智能开发/排查任务共用同一逻辑，均无模型选择入口。
  - 同步清理了前端 store 与 API 客户端里的模型选择状态和调用。

  验证

  - npx tsc --noEmit 通过
  - npm run build 通过
  - cargo build --release 两版通过
  - 已重新生成 static/dist/ 并重新打包 cc-web-dev.exe、cc-web-implCon.exe
  - 无头浏览器截图确认：聊天顶部只剩“Claude Code”助手选择，模型下拉已消失

  当前没有遗留的后台进程，改动尚未提交。

       99  - **分屏**：支持 1×1 / 2×1 / 1×2 / 2×2 / 3×2 / 3×3，同步模式把

  Thought for 19s, read 1 file, ran 4 shell commands (ctrl+o to expand)

● 已为两个版本加上会话批量管理（共享同一个 SessionList
  组件，开发版和实施版同时生效）。

  使用方式

  1. 在左侧「会话」区域，点击搜索框右侧的清单图标进入批量管理。
  2. 每个会话前出现复选框，点击整行也可以勾选/取消。
  3. 顶部管理栏显示：
     - 全选当前列表（会按当前搜索条件选择，而不是只选屏幕可见部分）
     - 已选 N
     - 删除 (N) 批量删除按钮
  4. 删除前有二次确认：确定删除选中的 N 个会话吗？
  5. 点击 × 退出批量管理并清空选择。

  实现细节

  - 支持部分失败：批量删除后提示“已删除 X 个，Y
    个删除失败”；已被别处删除（404）的会话视为成功，不会残留在列表。
  - 如果删除的是当前会话，会自动切到剩余列表中的下一个会话；没有剩余则回到空状态
    。
  - 批量删除会同时关闭正在运行的 SSE 流，并清理分屏面板中指向已删会话的引用。
  - 单个删除也复用同一套批量删除逻辑，行为保持一致。
  - 搜索过滤后进入批量管理时，全选只作用于当前过滤结果。

  验证

  - npx tsc --noEmit 通过
  - npm run build 通过
  - 已重新构建 cc-web-dev.exe 和 cc-web-implCon.exe
  - 无头浏览器实际验证：默认状态显示批量管理入口，进入后复选框、全选、计数、批量
    删除按钮布局正常

  改动尚未提交。

  单列表三种。其中按项目分组就是将每个产品环境变量相同的会话归为一组（没有产品  
  环境变量的分组归到未分组下，其余的分组名称显示对应的产品环境变量名称），按工  
  作区分组即将工作目录相同的会话归为一组（实施版没有这种方式），单列表分组即是  
  现在这样的。第二组是“排序方式”，提供手动排序和最近更新两种排序方式，手动排序  
  的逻辑需要你帮忙实现一下。 另外把每个会话前面的C图标去掉，鼠标悬浮到该会话上

  │ 将把“固定资产项目”从列表中移除。        │
  │ 文件夹与会话记录会保留，其会话将显示在  │
  │ “未分组”下。                           │
  │                              [取消] [移除] │
  └────────────────────────────────────────┘

  删除分组只影响左侧列表的展示，不删除会话、不删除工作目录。

  五、我准备这样实现「手动排序」

  - 选择「手动排序」后，会话行和分组标题都可以拖拽调整顺序。
  - 会话顺序全局保存；分组顺序按分组方式分别保存。
  - 新会话默认出现在所属分组顶部，之后可以手动拖动。
  - 切回「最近更新」时按修改时间倒序，拖拽禁用；再切回手动排序时恢复之前保存的顺
    序。
  - 顺序保存在浏览器 localStorage，重启 cc-web 后仍然有效。

  六、需要你确认的 4 个细节

  1. 按项目分组的匹配规则
     我计划：优先用节点运行记录里的产品环境快照匹配会话；普通会话则用
     session.cwd == 产品环境.code_directory 匹配，分组名显示产品环境的
     project_name。都匹配不到的归入「未分组」。这样可以吗？
  2. 分组重命名
     分组是从环境/目录实时算出来的，所以重命名我计划只保存一个本地显示别名，不改
     产品环境变量本身。可以吗？
  3. 删除分组
     删除分组我计划做成本地隐藏该分组，其会话归入「未分组」，并在视图菜单里提供
     「重置分组」恢复。可以吗？
  4. 文案范围
     这次只把侧边栏标题从「工作区」改回「会话」，还是把新建/重命名/删除弹窗里的
     「工作区」也一起改回「会话」？

  如果以上理解没问题，回复「确认」，我就按这个方案改代码。

  Thought for 56s, ran 8 shell commands (ctrl+o to expand)

● 已按你的确认实现完成。

  左侧会话区

  会话                    [视图] [＋] [批量] [搜索]

  - 标题恢复为「会话」。
  - 新建、批量、搜索、视图选项在同一行，视图按钮位于新建左侧。
  - 点击搜索后整行变为输入框，右侧 × 关闭并清空搜索。
  - 每个会话前的 C 图标已移除。
  - 鼠标悬浮会话或分组时，名称自动收缩截断，右侧显示「重命名」「删除」按钮。

  视图选项

  点击「视图」按钮后弹出两组选项：

  - 分组方式：按项目 / 按工作区 / 单列表
    - 实施版自动隐藏「按工作区」。
    - 按项目：优先用 session.cwd 匹配产品环境的代码目录，匹配不到时使用节点运行
      记录里的环境快照；分组名显示产品环境的
      project_name，都匹配不到的进入「未分组」。
    - 按工作区：按相同工作目录分组，分组名显示目录名。
    - 单列表：保持原来的列表形态。
  - 排序方式：手动排序 / 最近更新

  手动排序

  - 选择「手动排序」后，会话行和分组标题都可以拖拽。
  - 会话只能在同一个分组内拖动（分组是从项目/目录实时推导的，不能把会话拖进别的
    项目组）。
  - 会话顺序全局保存；分组顺序按分组方式分别保存。
  - 新会话默认出现在所属分组顶部。
  - 切回「最近更新」时按修改时间排序、禁用拖拽；再切回手动排序会恢复之前保存的顺
    序。
  - 单列表 + 手动排序时使用平铺列表，不显示“今天/昨天”日期标题，以便跨日期拖动。
  - 顺序保存在 localStorage，重启 cc-web 后仍保留。

  分组的重命名与删除

  - 分组重命名只保存本地显示别名，不改产品环境变量或目录本身。
  - 删除分组时按你的要求处理：
    - 不做分组级隐藏；
    - 不提供恢复/重置入口；
    - 把该组当前全部会话标记为「未分组」，会话记录和文件夹都保留；
    - 以后新建的、符合相同项目或目录条件的会话仍会正常形成分组。
  - 确认文案为：

  ▎ 将把“xxx”从列表中移除。文件夹与会话记录会保留，其会话将显示在“未分组”下。

  删除会话、批量删除也保留了确认提示。

  部署

  已重新构建并更新两个部署目录：

  - D:\project\cc-web-kaifa\cc-web-dist\cc-web.exe（开发版）
  - D:\project\cc-web-shishi\cc-web-dist\cc-web.exe（实施版）

  实施版已停止旧进程并重新启动，当前监听 3031；开发版目录已更新但未启动。前端
  tsc、npm run build 和两版 release 构建均通过，并用无头浏览器验证了默认列表、视
  图菜单和工作区分组布局。改动尚未提交。

  Thought for 31s, ran 5 shell commands (ctrl+o to expand)

● 已加上分组折叠。

  现在的行为

  - 每个分组标题左侧都有折叠箭头：
    - ▸ 折叠
    - ▾ 展开
  - 点击分组标题（或箭头）即可折叠/展开；也支持键盘 Enter / Space。
  - 折叠后只保留分组标题和会话数量，组内会话隐藏。
  - 分组名称悬浮时仍会截断，并显示重命名、删除按钮。
  - 折叠状态按分组方式分别保存到 localStorage，刷新页面或重启 cc-web 后保持。
  - 项目分组、工作区分组、日期分组、「未分组」都支持折叠。
  - 批量管理、搜索、手动排序、拖拽分组顺序均不受影响；折叠状态下计数仍然显示当前
    分组内的会话数。

  已更新部署

  - D:\project\cc-web-kaifa\cc-web-dist\cc-web.exe（开发版）
  - D:\project\cc-web-shishi\cc-web-dist\cc-web.exe（实施版）

  实施版已停止旧进程、替换 exe 并重新启动，当前监听
  3031；开发版目录已更新但未启动。

  验证通过：tsc、npm run build、两版 release 构建；无头浏览器确认了展开和折叠两
  种状态，折叠后仅保留分组标题与数量。改动尚未提交。

  Thought for 58s, ran 6 shell commands (ctrl+o to expand)

● 已修复。

  原因

  NodePromptsPanel 之前只在面板首次打开时请求一次
  /api/node-prompts，切换「开发版 /
  实施版」时只是在前端过滤已有数据，所以点击标签不会发出任何请求。

  修改

  切换版本时现在会：

  1. 关闭当前打开的编辑、预览、删除弹窗；
  2. 重新请求 GET /api/node-prompts；
  3. 按新选择的版本重新过滤列表。

  行为与旧版 npSwitchEdition 一致。接口本身仍返回全部版本数据，前端按 edition
  过滤。

  部署状态

  - 开发版：D:\project\cc-web-kaifa\cc-web-dist\cc-web.exe，当前正在运行（3030）
  - 实施版：D:\project\cc-web-shishi\cc-web-dist\cc-web.exe，已重启（3031）

  两个运行实例的 app.js 哈希都已确认与最新构建产物一致。tsc、npm run build、两版
  release 构建均通过。改动尚未提交。

  Thought for 1m 12s, ran 8 shell commands (ctrl+o to expand)

● 已修复智能开发提示词的版本切换问题，并重新构建、部署了两个实例。

  原因

  1. 切换「开发版 / 实施版」时，请求一直是同一个
     /api/node-prompts，没有携带版本参数，所以服务端返回同一批数据。
  2. 前端之前用严格条件 item.edition === edition
     过滤。如果服务端已经按版本过滤、返回的行里又没有 edition 字段，切到实施版就
     会被前端自己过滤成空列表，表现为“有响应但页面什么都没有”。

  修改

  - 请求改为携带版本参数：
    - 开发版：GET /api/node-prompts?edition=dev
    - 实施版：GET /api/node-prompts?edition=novice
  - 响应兼容两种服务端行为：
    - 行里有 edition 字段：前端按当前版本过滤；
    - 行里没有 edition 字段：认为服务端已按版本过滤，直接展示返回结果。
  - 返回结构兼容数组和 { items: [...] } 两种形式。
  - 空列表不再是一片空白，会明确显示：
    - 「暂无开发版提示词配置」
    - 或「暂无实施版提示词配置（接口返回的数据里没有该版本的记录）」

  部署

  两个实例已重新打包并重启：

  - 开发版 3030：D:\project\cc-web-kaifa\cc-web-dist\cc-web.exe
  - 实施版 3031：D:\project\cc-web-shishi\cc-web-dist\cc-web.exe

  两边 app.js 哈希均已确认与最新构建一致。

  需要注意：开发版当前连接的是 fast9.shenzhuo.vip
  的远程补丁中心。如果切换后实施版仍为空，但页面提示“接口返回的数据里没有该版本
  的记录”，说明该远程服务的数据库中没有实施版提示词，或不支持 edition
  参数；这就需要服务端补数据或升级，前端已经无法凭现有响应显示不存在的数据。

✻ Cogitated for 12m 10s · done 11:01

  修改内容

  - 去掉状态下拉框：不再按分析状态过滤，状态列仍保留用于展示。
  - 新增产品名称搜索框：支持直接输入，并带已有产品名称的自动提示（datalist）。
  - 新增版本号搜索框：输入产品后，会提示该产品已有版本。
  - 产品名称和版本号在普通检索和高级检索里共用同一份条件；无论是否打开高级搜索，
    都会随请求提交。
  - 「重置」会一并清空产品名称和版本号。
  - 普通检索行现在是：

  [产品名称] [版本号] [搜索补丁名称、描述、关键词…] [搜索] [高级搜索] [重置]

  1. 实施版视图分组恢复三种
     - 现在两种版本都支持：按项目、按工作区、单列表。
     - 实施版不再隐藏「按工作区」。
  2. 实施版补丁检索页
     - 右上角「创建任务」改为「返回」，点击回到智能开发视图。
  3. 实施版产品环境变量页
     - 顶部新增「返回」按钮，回到智能开发视图。
  4. 实施版设置
     - 新增「产品环境变量」页签，可直接在设置里查看、新增、编辑环境。
     - 设置页签现为：外观、产品环境变量、环境检查、关于。

     - 选择产品环境后，不再显示代码目录、home/war、JDK、录制页面等信息块。

     - 去掉了任务、产品环境、节点运行、登录、新建/重命名会话等表单里的
       placeholder 提示。
     - 截图/附件下方没有附件时不再显示虚线提示框。
     - 环境变量新增表单的「高级选项」也去掉了右侧说明文字。
  7. 创建会话下拉
     - 左侧「会话」区域点新建按钮，会弹出两个选项：
       - 创建普通会话：打开原来的选择工作目录创建会话。
       - 创建开发任务：
         - 开发版跳转到智能开发页并自动打开新建运行表单；
         - 实施版打开「创建排查任务」弹窗。
     - 两个版本都已接入。

✻ Cooked for 6m 24s · done 11:51

❯ 怎么配置启动时不自动打开浏览器                                                
  Thought for 20s, ran 1 shell command (ctrl+o to expand)

● 启动时不自动打开浏览器，程序本身已经支持两种方式：

  方式 1：启动参数（临时）

  cc-web.exe --no-browser

  现有 start.bat 会把参数透传给 exe，所以也可以：

  start.bat --no-browser

  方式 2：环境变量（推荐长期配置）

  设置：

  set CC_WEB_NO_BROWSER=1
  cc-web.exe

  支持的值：1、true、yes、on（不区分大小写）。

  如果希望双击 start.bat 也不打开浏览器

  修改对应目录的 start.bat，在启动 exe 前加一行即可：

  set "CC_WEB_NO_BROWSER=1"

  例如放到现有的 MCP 配置旁边：

  setlocal
  set "CC_WEB_MCP_CONFIG=%~dp0mcp-servers.json"
  set "CC_WEB_NO_BROWSER=1"

  两个部署目录分别修改：

  - D:\project\cc-web-kaifa\cc-web-dist\start.bat
  - D:\project\cc-web-shishi\cc-web-dist\start.bat

  其他启动方式

  - Linux / macOS：CC_WEB_NO_BROWSER=1 ./cc-web
  - systemd 服务：在 service 里加 Environment=CC_WEB_NO_BROWSER=1
  - Windows 快捷方式：目标改成 "...\cc-web.exe" --no-browser

  关闭自动打开浏览器不影响服务本身，之后手动访问对应地址即可：

  - 开发版：http://localhost:3030
  - 实施版：http://127.0.0.1:3031

  需要的话我可以直接帮你把两个 start.bat 都改成默认不自动打开浏览器。

✻ Cogitated for 5m 8s · done 12:49

❯ 能优化一下登录吗？做一个30天免登录的选项？你有什么方案                        

  Thought for 40s, ran 2 shell commands (ctrl+o to expand)

● 可以，而且现在确实适合把登录体验一起优化。先说现状和方案。

  现状

  - 登录由 patch_search 负责，cc-web 本身没有登录接口。
  - 当前 JWT access token 固定 120 分钟过期（patch_search/config.yaml 的
    access_token_expire_minutes: 120）。
  - 前端把 token 存在 localStorage，过期后 /api/auth/me 返回
    401，就会被清掉并回登录页。
  - 后端目前没有 refresh token，所以纯前端无法把登录延长到 30 天。

  方案 A：30 天有效期的 access token（简单、推荐先做）

  登录接口增加 remember_me 参数：

  { "username": "...", "password": "...", "remember_me": true }

  勾选「30 天免登录」时，后端签发有效期 30 天的 access token；不勾选时保持现在的
  2 小时。

  前端只增加：
  - 登录页一个「30 天内免登录」复选框；
  - 记住上次选择；
  - token 过期后仍按现有逻辑回登录页。

  优点：
  - 改动小，不需要数据库表、不需要 refresh 接口；
  - 现有 token_version、改密码失效机制仍然有效；
  - 对局域网/本机使用足够。

  缺点：
  - token 被拿到后 30 天内都有效；
  - 没有按设备撤销能力，只能靠改密码/token_version 让全部 token 失效。

  方案 B：短 access token + 30 天 refresh token（更安全）

  - access token 仍 2 小时；
  - 勾选免登录时，额外返回一个 30 天 refresh token；
  - 前端在 401 或快过期时自动调 /api/auth/refresh 换新 token，用户无感；
  - 退出登录/改密码时撤销 refresh token；
  - 可做轮换、设备列表、单设备踢下线。

  优点：
  - 被盗的 access token 只有 2 小时有效；
  - 可以按设备撤销，安全性明显更好。

  缺点：
  - 后端要加 refresh token 表、/refresh 接口、撤销逻辑；
  - 前端要加自动刷新、并发请求去重、刷新失败回登录；
  - 工作量明显更大。

  方案 C：只改服务端配置（最快，但不是“选项”）

  把 access_token_expire_minutes 直接改成 43200（30 天）。

  不用改代码，但会所有账号全部变成 30 
  天，没有勾选与否的区分。适合临时应急，不建议作为长期方案。

  我的建议

  如果这套系统主要在内网/本机使用：先做方案 A，成本低、立刻有「30
  天免登录」体验。

  如果后续会暴露到更广的网络、或者需要按设备撤销登录：做方案 B。

  需要确认的一点

  开发版当前连接的是远程 fast9.shenzhuo.vip 补丁中心，实施版连接本机
  127.0.0.1:13587。

  30 天免登录必须由补丁中心后端签发，所以：

  - 本机实施版可以直接改 D:\project\patch_search 并部署；
  - 开发版要生效，需要同步更新远程 fast9 那台补丁中心。

  请确认：

  1. 选方案 A 还是 B？
  2. 开发版连接的远程补丁中心你能一起更新吗？

  确认后我再开始改代码。

  新本机的即可

       94 +async def issue_refresh_token(                                
       95 +    settings,                                                 
       96 +    db,                                                       
       97 +    user: dict[str, Any],                                     
       98 +    user_agent: str | None = None,                            
       99 +) -> tuple[str, int]:                                         
      100 +    """签发 30 天 refresh token（明文只返回给客户端，库里只存 
          +sha256）。"""                                                 
      101 +    plain = secrets.token_urlsafe(48)                         
      102 +    days = _refresh_token_expire_days(settings)               
      103 +    await db.execute(                                         
      104 +        """                                                   
      105 +        INSERT INTO auth_refresh_token                        
      106 +            (user_id, token_hash, token_version, user_agent, e
          +xpires_at)                                                    
      107 +        VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, DATE_ADD(NOW(), INTERVAL %s DA
          +Y))                                                           
      108 +        """,

      110 +            int(user["id"]),                                  
      111 +            _refresh_token_hash(plain),

      114 +            days,

      117 +    # 顺手清理该用户已过期/已撤销很久的旧记录                 
      118 +    try:                                                      
      119 +        await db.execute(                                     
      120 +            """                                               
      121 +            DELETE FROM auth_refresh_token                    
      122 +             WHERE user_id=%s

      124 +                    OR (revoked_at IS NOT NULL AND revoked_at 
          +< DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 7 DAY)))                           
      125 +            """,                                              
      126 +            (int(user["id"]),),                               
      127 +        )                                                     
      128 +    except Exception:                                         
      129 +        pass                                                  
      130 +    return plain, days * 86400                                
      131 +

      133 +async def resolve_refresh_token(db, token: str) -> dict[str, A
          +ny] | None:                                                   
      134 +    """校验 refresh token，返回可用于签发 access token 的用户 
          +行。"""                                                       
      135 +    if not token:                                             
      136 +        return None                                           
      137 +    row = await db.fetch_one(                                 
      138 +        """                                                   
      139 +        SELECT rt.id AS refresh_id,                           
      140 +               rt.token_version AS refresh_token_version,     
      141 +               u.id, u.username, u.display_name, u.role, u.sta
          +tus, u.token_version                                          
      142 +          FROM auth_refresh_token rt                          
      143 +          JOIN user_account u ON u.id = rt.user_id            
      144 +         WHERE rt.token_hash=%s                               
      145 +           AND rt.revoked_at IS NULL                          
      146 +           AND rt.expires_at > NOW()                          
      147 +         LIMIT 1                                              
      148 +        """,                                                  
      149 +        (_refresh_token_hash(token),),                        
      150 +    )                                                         
      151 +    if not row:                                               
      152 +        return None                                           
      153 +    if int(row["status"]) != 1:                               
      154 +        return None                                           
      155 +    if int(row["token_version"]) != int(row["refresh_token_ver
          +sion"]):                                                      
      156 +        return None                                           
      157 +    await db.execute(                                         
      158 +        "UPDATE auth_refresh_token SET last_used_at=NOW() WHER
          +E id=%s",                                                     
      159 +        (int(row["refresh_id"]),),                            
      160 +    )                                                         
      161 +    return row                                                
      162 +                                                              
      163 +                                                              
      164 +async def revoke_refresh_token(db, token: str) -> None:       
      165 +    if not token:                                             
      166 +        return                                                
      167 +    await db.execute(                                         
      168 +        "UPDATE auth_refresh_token SET revoked_at=NOW() WHERE 
          +token_hash=%s AND revoked_at IS NULL",                        
      169 +        (_refresh_token_hash(token),),                        
      170 +    )                                                         
      171 +                                                              
      172 +                                                              
      173 +async def revoke_all_refresh_tokens(db, user_id: int) -> None:

      482 +/** 带自动续期的原始响应请求：401 时尝试 refresh token，再重试
          +一次。 */                                                     
      483 +export async function patchRawFetch(                          
      484 +  path: string,                                               
      485 +  init: RequestInit = {}                                      
      486 +): Promise<Response> {                                        
      487 +  let response = await fetchWithPatchServers(path, init);     
      488 +  if (response.status !== 401) return response;               
      489 +  try {                                                       
      490 +    const accessToken = await refreshAccessToken();           
      491 +    if (!accessToken) return response;                        
      492 +    response = await fetchWithPatchServers(path, init);       
      493 +  } catch (error) {                                           
      494 +    // refresh token 本身失效：原 401 交给调用方统一处理；网络
          +问题直接抛出，避免误清登录态。                                
      495 +    if (error instanceof ApiError && (error.status === 401 || 
          +error.status === 403)) {                                      
      496 +      return response;                                        
      497 +    }                                                         
      498 +    throw error;                                              
      499 +  }                                                           
      500 +  return response;                                            
      501 +}                                                             
      502 +                                                              
      503 +export async function patchFetch<T = unknown>(                
      504 +  path: string,                                               
      505 +  init: RequestInit = {}                                      
      506 +): Promise<T> {                                               
      507 +  const response = await patchRawFetch(path, init);           
      508 +  if (!response.ok) {                                         
      509 +    if (response.status === 401) {                            
      510 +      expireSession();                                        
      511 +      throw new ApiError('登录已失效，请重新登录', response.st
          +atus);                                                        
      512 +    }                                                         
      513 +    if (response.status === 403) throw new ApiError('权限不足'
          +, response.status);                                           
      514 +    throw new ApiError(await parseError(response), response.st
          +atus);                                                        
      515 +  }                                                           
      516 +  const text = await response.text();                         
      517 +  if (!text) return undefined as T;                           
      518 +  try {                                                       
      519 +    return JSON.parse(text) as T;                             
      520 +  } catch {                                                   
      521 +    return text as unknown as T;                              
      522 +  }                                                           
      523 +}
